**[00:03:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000008)  
Today, as we scrutinize the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of Budget and Management and the special purpose funds, we do so in light of the recent landmark decision of the Supreme Court concerning the constitutionality of certain provisions under previous and present General Appropriations Acts. Bagamat hindi dineklara ng Supreme Court na unconstitutional ang unprogrammed appropriations, malinaw ang naging paalala nito sa Kongreso. We have a constitutional duty to exercise fiscal discipline,

**[00:04:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000009)  
uphold transparency and accountability, and ensure that every appropriation complies with the mandates of our Constitution. Malinaw po sa inyong lingkod at sa committee na ito ang ating tungkulin. We must ensure that the national budget is not only fiscally sound and responsive to the needs of our people, but also fully compliant with the Constitution. In its decision, the Supreme Court reminded us

**[00:04:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000010)  
of the fundamental responsibility that comes with the power of the purse. Every peso collected in taxes and fees represents labor rendered, needs deferred, perseverance and sacrifice endured by our people. The General Appropriations Act [unclear] that sacrifice. This should guide us throughout our deliberations and remind us that the power of appropriations is not without limits, especially in light of the provisions provided in the 1987 Constitution.

**[00:05:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000011)  
Kaya naman po mahalaga ang papel ng DBM sa landmark desisyon na ito. Bukod sa 2.9 bilyon na pondo ng ahensya, ang DBM ay responsable din sa management ng higit-kumulang 550 billion na special purpose funds at unprogrammed appropriations. Nais nating malaman ngayong araw: una, ano ang implikasyon ng desisyon ng Korte Suprema sa binabalangkas nating national budget para sa Fiscal Year 2027?

**[00:05:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000012)  
Pangalawa, anong adjustments ang kailangan nating baguhin sa kasalukuyang version ng budget upang maiwasan ang anumang constitutional infirmities? Our responsibility does not end with determining how much the government may spend. We must also ensure that public funds are appropriated for clearly defined purposes, supported by available resources, and subject to proper safeguards and accountability.

**[00:08:10.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000028)  
For fiscal year 2027, the Department of Budget and Management is proposing 3.125 billion to support public financial management, procurement reform, digital transformation, transparency, and organizational efficiency.

**[00:08:28.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000029)  
This may be a relatively modest budget compared with those of our largest departments, but the DBM carries a mandate that cuts across the entire bureaucracy.

**[00:08:39.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000030)  
Its work affects practically every department, every program, and every peso in the national budget.

**[00:08:47.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000031)  
At the heart of its mandate is a simple principle. Every peso of public money must be properly allocated, prudently spent, transparently managed, and fully accounted for.

**[00:08:58.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000032)  
The DBM pursues this through three pillars. Fiscal discipline, effective resource allocation, and efficient government operations.

**[00:09:08.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000033)

**[00:09:18.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000034)

**[00:30:02.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000559)  
For the contingent fund, we are proposing to allocate 13 billion pesos which is the same level as in 2025 and 2026.

**[00:30:11.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000560)  
This fund covers funding requirements of new or urgent activities or projects of NGAs, GOCCs, and LGUs that need to be implemented or paid during the year, such as but not limited to the following.

**[00:30:26.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000561)  
Legal obligation of the government arising from final and executory decisions of competent authorities, such as compromise agreements, arbitral awards, mediation settlement agreement, and professional services in connection thereto, requirements of newly created offices, or deficiencies in the appropriations for local and external travels of the President of the Philippines.

**[00:30:50.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000562)  
In no case shall it be used for the following.

**[00:30:53.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000563)  
The provision of confidential and intelligence fund to civilian organizations.

**[00:30:56.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000564)  
Departments and agencies with no mandate to perform security and intelligence-related functions, unless the President certifies as to the extreme necessity thereof, and for the purchase of motor vehicles, including any improvements thereon.

**[00:31:13.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000565)  
The release from the contingent fund is subject to the prior approval of the President of the Philippines, except for the payment resulting from final and executory decisions of competent authorities.

**[00:31:23.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000566)

as of end of third quarter, total allotments released to various implementing agencies amounts to

**[00:31:33.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000568)  
16.35 billion, thereby leaving a balance of 3.13 billion pesos.

**[00:31:43.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000569)  
The balance is comprised of 2.31 billion from 2026 GAA and the remaining 820 million from the continuing appropriations of 2025.

**[00:44:47.580] MMDA Chairman Romando Artes** (asr-000951)  
And

RGAA is

**[00:45:22.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000961)

**[00:45:38.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000964)  
Yes, my first question will probably be regarding Falgo because in a reduced budget, it is a distinct increase.

**[00:45:50.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000965)  
What's the policy in the Falgo retaining congressional increases?

**[00:45:54.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000966)  
What justified the increase? Falgo was originally proposed at 5 billion.

**[00:45:59.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000967)  
In the 2026 NEP, increased by 41.845 billion in the House GAAP, enacted at 37.493 in the 2026 GAA.

**[00:46:12.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000968)  
The enacted amount represents a 32.493 billion increase over the NEP, which was retained in the 2027.

**[00:46:21.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000969)  
As we understand, the usual policy of DBM, yung congressional increases, tinatanggal,

**[00:46:29.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000970)  
sa panibagong budget sa susunod na taon, reverting the allocations to their originally proposed levels.

**[00:46:37.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000971)  
Bakit dito nag-iba?

**[00:46:40.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000972)  
Imbes natanggalin, bakit rinitain yung congressional increase noong 2026 sa Falgo?

**[00:46:46.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000973)  
Is this an exception? And were the same criteria applied?

**[00:46:51.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000974)  
Bakit ninyo rinitain na dati-dati talaga tinatanggal yan, diba? Binabalik sa dating level ng NEP?

**[00:46:59.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000975)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, the principal reason for the increase in the allocation for Falgo is brought about by the request as well from the various leagues of local government units.

**[00:47:10.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000976)  
And in 2026, we saw the implementation of the Bawat Bayan Makikinabang Program of the President under the Falgo.

**[00:47:19.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000977)  
And it is the first time under the history of Falgo where all LGUs, municipalities, cities, and provinces actually receive an allocation.

**[00:47:29.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000979)  
From the Falgo, covering among others the distribution of rice for our fellow citizens.

**[00:47:36.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000980)  
So that was also the same policy that was carried over for 2027.

**[00:47:41.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000981)  
So that means the rice distribution program under the Falgo will be carried over to 2027 as well, citing the implementation that we have had in 2026.

**[00:47:52.900] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000982)  
So we intend to pursue that for 2027.

**[00:47:56.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000983)  
Hence, we retained the level...

**[00:47:59.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000984)  
Level approved under the GAA for the 2027 to sustain the implementation of the Bawat Bayan Makikinabang Program, Your Honor.

**[00:48:07.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000985)  
Nako, Secretary, kapag nakinig kayo sa lahat ng request ng politiko, patay kang bata ka.

**[00:48:13.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000986)  
At bakit naging mas politiko pa ang BBM kesa sa amin?

**[00:48:18.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000987)  
Dahil as of 15 August 2026, only 1.606 billion or 4.3 of the 2026 appropriation has actually been obligated.

**[00:48:29.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000988)  
Tapos yung disbursement, ang baba-baba pati, nasa 1.45.973 million.

**[00:48:36.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000989)  
So what justifies this humongous increase in the allocation from 2026 to 2027 na 5 billion lang dati, lumobo na sa 37.493.

**[00:48:51.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000990)  
Samantalang hindi naman pala nagagastos ang baba-baba ng implementation.

**[00:48:55.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000991)  
Di ba yan parati ninyo sinisita sa amin?

**[00:48:58.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000992)  
Hindi nag-iimplement.

**[00:48:59.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000993)  
Hindi nag-iimplement itong mga ahensya at hindi pwede natin i-increase.

**[00:49:03.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000994)  
Identify please the projects and explain.

**[00:49:06.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000995)  
Ang baba ng implementation, ba't kayo nagdagdag ng napakalaki?

**[00:49:12.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000996)  
From 5 billion, naging 37.493.

**[00:49:16.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000997)  
Paano himala yan?

**[00:49:18.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000998)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, again we note that almost half of the amount appropriated for Falgo went to the rice distribution program.

**[00:49:26.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-000999)  
And we recognize the figures.

**[00:49:28.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001000)  
And we recognize the figures stated by the Honorable Marcos on the implementation.

**[00:49:32.660] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001001)  
These may be the figures from the monitoring of the DILG of the implementation of the Falgo.

**[00:49:38.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001002)  
Hindi palagas ninyo, DBM2.

**[00:49:41.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001003)  
Oo.

**[00:49:42.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001004)  
Nasa 4.3 lang.

**[00:49:43.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001005)  
Because it was generated, Your Honor, from the actual LGU-reported obligation and disbursement under the Subay Bayan Portal.

**[00:49:51.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001006)  
Okay. Ganito na lang, Secretary.

**[00:49:53.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001007)  
Identify the projects and LGUs provided assistance under the 2025 and 2027.

**[00:49:58.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001008)  
FAA Appropriation for Falgo.

**[00:50:02.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001009)  
Yung FAA.

**[00:50:04.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001010)  
Bigay na lang ninyo listahan bago mag-plenary kami kung talagang handa ba sila mag-implement and the measures needed to prevent another backlog.

**[00:50:14.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001011)  
4.3, entering the last quarter is a miserable figure.

**[00:50:20.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001012)  
You know that better than I.

**[00:50:21.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001013)  
Kayo nagmo-monitor dyan.

**[00:50:23.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001014)  
So yung next question ko.

**[00:50:25.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001015)  
With the indulgence of Senator Marcos.

**[00:50:27.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001016)  
With that.

**[00:50:27.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001017)  
Para nang hindi naulitin mamaya.

**[00:50:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001018)  
Kasi nagiging magandang.

**[00:50:32.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001019)  
It will be nice if this can be addressed as Secretary de Leon.

**[00:50:37.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001020)  
That itong.

**[00:50:39.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001021)  
They view the LGSF now as the port.

**[00:50:41.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001022)  
The new port.

**[00:50:43.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001023)  
Para mawala na lang po yung ganong agam-agam.

**[00:50:46.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001024)  
It's the DBM that nagpo-process.

**[00:50:50.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001025)  
Nag-revet supposedly.

**[00:50:51.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001026)  
So can you just tell us what are the criteria, factors, parameters?

**[00:50:57.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001027)  
BAU.

**[00:50:57.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001028)  
In determining which LGUs receive more support, ilang porsyento ng ating mga LGUs ang nakinabang sa LGSF allocation

**[00:51:05.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001029)  
para makita natin kung may pinapaboran ba o talagang lahat naman ay napagbibigyan.

**[00:51:11.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001030)  
So can you address this issue once and for all para malaman natin kung ano yung criteria, factors, and parameters.

**[00:51:20.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001031)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:51:21.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001032)  
Your Honor.

**[00:51:21.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001033)  
We would gladly share the parameters we are using.

**[00:51:27.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001034)  
In terms of processing the release for LGUs.

**[00:51:30.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001035)  
But to the Honorable Senator Marcos' request for the list, we will certainly provide the list of recipients of FALGO for 2025 and 2026.

**[00:51:40.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001036)  
But as of right now, the list is also available online through the DBM Compass.

**[00:51:45.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001037)  
But we'll certainly furnish the list to the committee and to the Senator's office.

**[00:51:50.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001038)  
For the criteria that we use, the DBM looks at certain parameters in releasing the funds to our...

**[00:51:57.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001039)  
Number one, good financial housekeeping.

**[00:52:01.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001040)  
Second is compliance with the reporting requirements.

**[00:52:05.160] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001041)  
As we have said, we are working with the DILG to make sure that the LGUs are using the funds in accordance with the purpose for which it was released.

**[00:52:15.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001042)  
That is why we also have the Subay Bayan portal that is up and live right now where our fellow citizens can actually see the status of the projects funded by the FALGO.

**[00:52:25.920] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001043)  
We also look at...

**[00:52:27.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001044)  
Prior accomplishment, both physical and financial, in terms of their utilization of the past LGSF FALGO releases.

**[00:52:37.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001045)  
As to the question on reference on the LGSF as pork, may we note that in the Belgica versus Ochoa case, which declared the pork barrel system unconstitutional,

**[00:52:51.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001046)  
there's a decision that actually...

**[00:52:54.320] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001047)  
Decision by the Supreme Court in Belgica versus Executive Secretariat.

**[00:52:57.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001048)  
That actually upheld the constitutionality of the LGSF under the 2014 GAA, recognizing its discernible singular appropriation purpose of providing funds for supporting local governments.

**[00:53:13.400] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001049)  
The court further ruled that the LGSF satisfied the two requirements, which is completeness and sufficiency standard test.

**[00:53:20.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001050)  
But as we have mentioned at the House of Representatives, we remain committed to working with Congress.

**[00:53:26.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001051)  
If there is a need to introduce additional measures and safeguards to make sure that the local government support fund complies with this jurisprudence of the Supreme Court, Mr. Chair or Honor.

**[00:53:37.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001052)  
Thank you, Secretary De Leon.

**[00:53:38.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001053)  
Before Senator Marcos, I'd just like to acknowledge the presence of Senator Pangilinan.

**[00:53:43.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001054)  
Thank you. Please proceed, Senator Marcos.

**[00:53:45.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001055)  
Having said that, at lahat naman tayo kilala yung Belgica versus Ochoa,

**[00:53:53.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001056)  
ang tinatanong ko lang,

**[00:53:55.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001057)  
bakit napaka-baba ng implementasyon?

**[00:53:58.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001058)  
Ang baba-baba ng implementation.

**[00:54:00.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001059)  
What is it actually now?

**[00:54:01.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001060)  
Sinasabi mo, half went to the rise.

**[00:54:04.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001061)  
Pero actually, ang nasa libro ninyo, nasa 4.3 lang,

**[00:54:07.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001062)  
ang na-obligate.

**[00:54:10.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001063)  
Tapos mas mababa pa dun yung nadidisperse.

**[00:54:13.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001064)  
So ano ba talaga yung totoo?

**[00:54:15.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001065)  
Ba't ang baba-baba higit sa lahat?

**[00:54:17.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001066)  
At bakit tayo nagdadagdag kung hindi naman natin na-implement?

**[00:54:20.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001067)  
Kayo ang makulit dyan eh, kung tutuusin.

**[00:54:25.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001068)  
And we look at really the bottom line why the implementation of the LGSF

**[00:54:30.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001069)  
went a little bit slow for this time.

**[00:54:34.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001070)  
Half of the funds went to rise and in fact…

**[00:54:37.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001071)  
Maling rise kasi napakaraming rise.

**[00:54:39.160] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001072)  
It's the rise distribution program.

**[00:54:40.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001073)  
Ito po yung rise distribution program under the LGSF.

**[00:54:47.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001074)  
Ito po yung under the banner of Bawat Bayan Makikinabang.

**[00:54:50.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001075)  
Ito po yung program rin ng LGSF.

**[00:54:54.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001076)  
Maliban pa sa…

**[00:54:55.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001077)  
Billion-billion sa DA.

**[00:54:57.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001078)  
That is correct, Your Honor.

**[00:54:58.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001079)  
Tapos may rise program din.

**[00:55:02.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001080)  
Ito ba yung rise for all program or iba pa to?

**[00:55:05.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001081)  
Ang rise for all program, Your Honor, ito po yung under ng DA.

**[00:55:09.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001082)  
And ito naman, yung LGSF price, magkano yan?

**[00:55:16.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001083)  
Grabing kadami naman ng rise.

**[00:55:17.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001084)  
I'm just getting the actual amount, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[00:55:23.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001085)  
But the rise program under the LGSF,

**[00:55:25.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001086)  
ito po yung ang intention niya is to provide free rise allocation

**[00:55:29.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001087)  
para sa bottom 25% of the population.

**[00:55:33.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001088)  
As of October 15, from that LGSF amount,

**[00:55:37.660] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001089)  
22.96 billion have been released to various LGUs

**[00:55:41.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001090)  
for the implementation of the rise distribution program.

**[00:55:44.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001091)  
So, ito sa DA, hiwalay pa rin sa DSWD.

**[00:55:48.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001092)  
This is directly released to the LGUs, Your Honor.

**[00:55:51.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001093)  
For the record lang, Secretary,

**[00:55:53.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001094)  
magkano na ako na-disperse natin for the rise distribution program under the LGSF?

**[00:56:00.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001095)  
Tama.

**[00:56:04.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001096)  
That's correct.

**[00:56:05.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001097)  
Insofar as the national government, Your Honor,

**[00:56:08.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001098)  
we book recording when the funds are transferred to the LGUs.

**[00:56:12.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001099)  
So, it's the 22 billion that's already released to the LGUs.

**[00:56:15.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001100)  
Almost 23 is actually in the LGU na.

**[00:56:19.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001101)  
Wala na yan sa inyo talaga.

**[00:56:21.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001102)  
That is correct, Your Honor.

**[00:56:22.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001103)  
It's downloaded.

**[00:56:23.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001104)  
It's not obligated or disbursed.

**[00:56:25.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001105)  
Ano na siya?

**[00:56:26.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001106)  
On the books of the national government, Your Honor,

**[00:56:28.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001107)  
it's both obligated and disbursed to the local government.

**[00:56:31.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001108)  
O, sige. So, wala na kayo sa 4.3?

**[00:56:35.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001109)  
Probably, Your Honor, the 4.3 is the aggregate implementation of the LGUs themselves.

**[00:56:41.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001110)  
Hindi ko alam.

**[00:56:42.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001111)  
After nanggaring rin sa inyo yung figure na yan,

**[00:56:45.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001112)  
kaya ako nagtataka eh.

**[00:56:46.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001113)  
Tapos dadagdagan ng limpak-limpak.

**[00:56:48.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001114)  
Nakakasindak naman talaga.

**[00:56:50.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001115)  
In any case, I'd like to proceed to another...

**[00:56:53.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001116)  
which already has been raised by our chairman.

**[00:56:57.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001117)  
Ito yung nakakabiglang desisyon ng Supreme Court

**[00:57:02.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001118)  
determining the unconstitutionality of both the GAA 2024 and 2025.

**[00:57:09.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001119)  
The doctrine, obviously, of operative fact is in play.

**[00:57:14.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001120)  
And Justice Leono, who penned the decision,

**[00:57:17.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001121)  
invoked that together with good faith

**[00:57:19.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001122)  
and asserting no blanket shield for officials.

**[00:57:23.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001123)  
Who condoned such releases.

**[00:57:26.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001124)  
And the accountability is reasserted over these funds.

**[00:57:31.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001125)  
So, criminal, administrative, and civil cases can follow this decision.

**[00:57:37.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001126)  
I understand it will be appealed by the executive.

**[00:57:39.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001127)  
Nevertheless, what is very, very clear is that

**[00:57:42.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001128)  
unexpended amounts from the budget of 2024 to 2025,

**[00:57:48.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001129)  
unexpended or unspent amounts can no longer be disbursed.

**[00:57:53.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001130)  
Ano ang pagkaintindi ninyo yan?

**[00:57:55.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001131)  
What is the scope?

**[00:57:57.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001132)  
Because the dispositive portion identifies specific congressional increases.

**[00:58:04.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001133)  
Nakita natin yan.

**[00:58:05.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001134)  
And declares unconstitutional, consequently, the entire budget.

**[00:58:12.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001135)  
Anong intindi ninyo yan?

**[00:58:13.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001136)  
Does the DBM interpret this as the entire GAA of both years

**[00:58:19.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001137)  
becoming unconstitutional?

**[00:58:21.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001138)  
The unprogrammed appropriations?

**[00:58:23.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001139)  
Or only those congressional increases that were identified in the decision?

**[00:58:31.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001140)  
Are you interpreting narrowly, or moderately, or liberally?

**[00:58:36.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001141)  
Samba tayo.

**[00:58:37.860] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001142)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, actually over the past two nights,

**[00:58:42.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001143)  
the DBM-EXECOM has been carefully reviewing the Supreme Court's decision

**[00:58:47.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001144)  
to assess not only its legal, but also the fiscal, technical, and operational implications.

**[00:58:52.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001145)  
And to determine whether or not the DBM-EXECOM is going to be unconstitutional,

**[00:58:53.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001146)  
and to determine the appropriate steps and next steps.

**[00:58:56.900] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001147)  
As mentioned by the Honorable Marcos,

**[00:58:59.440] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001148)  
in fact, that's one of our points for discussion.

**[00:59:02.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001149)  
With the reference to the declaration of unconstitutionality

**[00:59:09.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001150)  
as a consequence of the entire 2025 GAA,

**[00:59:13.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001151)  
which remains valid up to now because it's continuing appropriation.

**[00:59:17.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001152)  
Tama.

**[00:59:17.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001153)  
We are still...

**[00:59:18.160] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001154)  
Malawan toon yan eh, dito.

**[00:59:18.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001155)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[00:59:19.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001156)  
So, pasok pa rin ng 2026.

**[00:59:21.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001157)  
We are discussing and probably discussing,

**[00:59:23.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001158)  
this is one of the items as mentioned by the Office of the Solicitor General

**[00:59:27.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001159)  
that we will be seeking clarification from the Supreme Court

**[00:59:31.400] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001160)  
because there are a lot of operational issues that may arise from this.

**[00:59:36.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001161)  
There are unreleased appropriations.

**[00:59:38.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001162)  
Ang dami pang unreleased, particularly yung 2025.

**[00:59:42.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001163)  
There are also available still.

**[00:59:44.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001164)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[00:59:45.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001165)  
There are also unobligated allotments on the part of the agencies.

**[00:59:49.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001166)  
And there are also unpaid obligations.

**[00:59:51.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001167)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[00:59:52.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001168)  
So, these are the things that we are...

**[00:59:53.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001169)  
listing down and we'll be closely coordinating with the Office of the Solicitor General.

**[01:00:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001170)  
the DBM, hihintuin muna lahat

**[01:00:02.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001171)  
ng bayarin

**[01:00:03.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001172)  
dahil hindi tayo sigurado

**[01:00:06.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001173)  
we're in limbo. Medyo

**[01:00:07.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001174)  
alanganin, what legal basis if any

**[01:00:10.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001175)  
will you have for further releases

**[01:00:12.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001176)

Kasi hindi namin

alam kung alin ba

**[01:14:58.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001733)  
talaga yun.

**[01:15:08.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001737)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair, we will furnish the Senator the detailed utilization of the N-Program.

**[01:15:15.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001738)  
Kababawan lang ito. Itatanong ko lang kasi binakita mo dito sa accomplishment ng first semester.

**[01:15:21.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001739)  
Meron lang kayong continuing appropriation of 598.28 million.

**[01:15:27.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001740)  
I assume this is from 2025. This continues to be available for obligation up to the end of the year.

**[01:15:51.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001741)  
Bakit 30.79% lang na-obligate?

**[01:15:56.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001742)  
184 million.

**[01:15:57.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001743)  
184 million pa lang ang nga na-o-obligate.

**[01:16:00.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001744)  
What's the obligation figure that you have that's best updated?

**[01:16:05.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001745)  
Your Honor, we confirmed the figure 598.28 under continuing appropriations.

**[01:16:12.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001746)  
Although we would like to submit na most of these...

**[01:16:15.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001747)  
Pero yung second to the last column, kayo rin nagbigay. 30.77 lang ang obligation.

**[01:16:20.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001748)  
Ba't hindi ninyo ginagastos?

**[01:16:21.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001749)  
That is correct, Your Honor. We reviewed the projects that are covered by these...

**[01:16:27.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001750)  
Continuing appropriations. In fact, yung iba po dito ay na-offer na doon po sa savings under the National Budget Circular 602 and 603.

**[01:16:39.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001751)  
So, kailangan lang po itong ma-update kasi yung mga negative saros po were issued after the cut-off of the reporting, Your Honor.

**[01:16:48.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001752)  
Nanghihinayang lang kami. Sayang yung pondo.

**[01:16:51.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001753)  
I-re-revert ba ito to Treasury? Nasa fourth quarter na tayo.

**[01:16:55.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001754)  
Ano bang programs and projects funded by the Treasury?

**[01:16:57.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001755)  
By this continuing approach. At bakit hindi magastos?

**[01:17:02.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001756)  
Your Honor, as mentioned, kami rin po nanghihinayang.

**[01:17:07.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001757)  
That's why, yun nga po, nagaya po nang nabanggit ko, in-offer po namin ito as savings para makatulong po doon sa ating uplift intervention.

**[01:17:15.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001758)  
That is also the same reason why, Your Honor, the DBM's budget is lower in 2027.

**[01:17:21.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001759)  
Dahil alam po namin na yung 2026 po namin, meron pa kaming magiging continuing.

**[01:17:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001760)  
So we are really looking at re-priority.

**[01:17:29.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001761)  
Ano ba talaga ito? Can you give us an idea? Kagilagilalas yung hindi nagagastos yung pera, naging bato pa.

**[01:17:36.900] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001762)  
Meron po tayo, Your Honor, na nakitang ini-implement natin na several projects under the Public Financial Management Program of the DBM.

**[01:17:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001763)  
At ito po ay kabilang sa ating mga nire-review. Na bakit nga po ba hindi ito ma-implement?

**[01:17:53.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001764)  
Sa tinirin ninyo ang recipient nito.

**[01:17:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001765)  
Precisely, Your Honor. So we're also doing internal review and housekeeping in terms of our own projects, Your Honor.

**[01:18:04.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001766)  
We are checking whether kailangan po ba talaga itong mga projects na ito na maipagpatuloy.

**[01:18:09.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001767)  
Hindi. Umabot na tayo ng halos November. Hindi pa nagagastos.

**[01:18:14.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001768)  
Again, Your Honor, that is precisely the reason behind us approving a lower budget for DBM for 2027.

**[01:18:21.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001769)  
Pagkatapos sa with the Supreme Court decision, baka kasama na ito.

**[01:18:26.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001770)  
Talagang lalahatin ang unreleased portions.

**[01:18:30.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001771)  
Isa po yun sa bahagi ng clarification na paglilino po na ating hihingin po patungkol sa desisyon, Your Honor.

**[01:18:36.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001772)  
Okay. Marami na akong tanong. Maraming salamat. Magsusunod na round na lang.

**[01:18:43.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001773)  
Thank you, Sector Marcos, our Senate President. You may already ask your questions.

**[01:18:48.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001774)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:18:53.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001775)  
Mr. Chairman, I think ang pinaka...

**[01:18:56.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001776)  
...news of the day ngayon, yung Supreme Court ruling.

**[01:19:00.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001777)  
And in fact, the Supreme Court ruling gave some directions and clarity, pero marami rin mga questions pagdating sa crafting of the budget.

**[01:19:15.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001778)  
I'll ask the questions later on. Pero I want to ask the Secretary, with this Supreme Court ruling on the unprogrammed funds,

**[01:19:26.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001779)  
and narinig ko kanina sa nag-umpisa na kayo and your team to go through the Supreme Court ruling.

**[01:19:34.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001780)  
Makapal siya. I also went through it or tried to go through it this morning.

**[01:19:39.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001781)  
But what can you now... We're now in the thick of the budget process.

**[01:19:44.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001782)  
In fact, sabi nga ng ating chairman, patapos na kami and we're going to enter into consolidation of the different budgets...

**[01:19:56.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001783)  
...of different agencies. And then from there, magpa-plenary debates na kami.

**[01:20:02.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001784)  
So what are the recommendations of the DBM in relation to the Supreme Court ruling so that we can already fine-tune, we can already adjust, we can already watch out for kung merong mga kailangan kaming tignan mabuti.

**[01:20:25.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001785)  
Okay.

**[01:20:26.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001786)  
Ang dapat naming i-adjust dito sa 2027 budget process.

**[01:20:34.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001787)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Mr. Senate President.

**[01:20:37.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001788)  
There are things that are indeed very clear in the decision.

**[01:20:41.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001789)  
And we are also happy to report that for one, when the Supreme Court ruled on the unconstitutionality of the SAGIP, we don't have SAGIP anymore even in the 2027 unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:20:55.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001790)  
Okay.

**[01:21:18.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001872)  
Because the amount we arrived at for that particular entry, meron naman pong pinanggalingan ng mga pipeline projects. So we stand ready to help Congress in complying with that guardrail by giving the breakdown kung paano po ba namin nakuha yung support to foreign assisted projects.

**[01:21:35.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001873)  
We have also, as we have reported, listed down fewer items sa ating UA for 2027. Yun nga lang po, Mr. Chair, Mr. Senate President, there are just some items which I think is also crucial in the budget legislation phase, particularly the declaration on the unconstitutionality of one of the triggers for unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:22:03.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001874)  
But again, we reserve, as earlier pronounced, how this can be best interpreted and applied moving forward. But one thing we can assure is the Executive is not releasing anything as of now triggered by Sections 1A and 1B as covered by the Supreme Court decision.

**[01:22:24.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001875)  
We also welcome if Congress, as mentioned by the Supreme Court decision, will include more details or suggestions.

**[01:22:33.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001876)  
Or safeguards or requirements that will guide the Executive in further executing the unprogrammed appropriations. Maybe more parameters if necessary para po makumply natin yung four guardrails as stated by the Supreme Court.

**[01:22:48.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001877)  
Because we note that there was also a provision in the decision, a reference about approving authority. So we submit to the wisdom of Congress and the Senate as well in further defining these conditions, Your Honor.

**[01:23:03.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001878)  
So basically, ang sinasabi ng Supreme Court is hindi pwedeng lumampas ang Kongreso doon sa NEP proposal ng Presidente for programmed and unprogrammed.

**[01:23:20.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001879)  
I think that's bottom line. That is one of the most important direction. And of course, nakasulat rin doon yung mga transparency.

**[01:23:32.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001880)  
Dapat matrack mabuti.

**[01:23:33.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001881)  
We also engage civil society movements in the budget, which we have done in the 2026 budget. In fact, lahat yan ay uploaded sa website. And we also engage civil society organizations.

**[01:23:44.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001882)  
And patuloy namin sini-engage. In fact, kahapon sa hearing ni Sen. Pangilina, nandun yung civil society organizations sharing their analysis.

**[01:23:56.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001883)  
So it's not only us analyzing, but we also engage third-party analysis through the civil society.

**[01:24:03.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001884)  
So bottom line, hindi pwedeng lumampas ang Kongreso sa proposal ng Presidente, both unprogrammed and programmed. Bottom line.

**[01:24:14.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001885)  
That is how we view the decision, Your Honor.

**[01:24:19.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001886)  
But what is clear right now, even unprogrammed.

**[01:24:23.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001887)  
Kasama po, Your Honor.

**[01:24:24.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001888)  
Even unprogrammed. Kasi that was the debate.

**[01:24:27.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001889)  
Unprogrammed, hindi nga lumalampas. Pero yung unprogrammed, sobrang-sobrang na lumalampas.

**[01:24:33.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001890)  
For example, in 2024, ang NEP 281, unprogrammed to, 281, biglang naging 731 yung final sa unprogrammed.

**[01:24:48.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001891)  
In 2025, ganun rin, 158 billion yung NEP. Pagdating sa final is 363.

**[01:24:57.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001892)  
So it's only itong 2026 na correct. Ang proposal is 249.

**[01:25:03.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001893)  
Ang final is 150. So bumaba sa proposal ng Presidente.

**[01:25:10.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001894)  
I'll go to some specifics because this is important in the way we will craft the budget.

**[01:25:16.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001895)  
Doon sa decision ng Supreme Court, pinagbawal niya o ginawa niyang unconstitutional yung special provision 1A and 1B.

**[01:25:32.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001896)  
Ang iniwan lang.

**[01:25:33.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001897)  
So ang ibig sabihin niyan, dito sa unprogrammed na gagawin for 2027, FAPS lang ang matitira.

**[01:25:45.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001898)  
Tama? Actually, nawala na yung essence of new revenue and new taxes. Nawala na yun.

**[01:25:52.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001899)  
Ang sinasabi, the way we understand it, in fact, I'm consulting our LBRMO, the way we understand it, foreign-assisted projects na lang ang matitira.

**[01:26:01.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001900)  
So, kanina.

**[01:26:03.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001901)  
Sa presentation ninyo, meron pang restoration ng PDIC, merong conversion of national government agencies sa unprogrammed. Lahat yung mawawala na.

**[01:26:14.920] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001902)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, the way we understood and the way we read, again, subject to the alignment later on with the Office of the Solicitor General,

**[01:26:23.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001903)  
meron pa naman pong possibility for that but ang sabi po sa decision, the reference is dapat yung excess is from the totality of the revenue.

**[01:26:32.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001904)  
What was particularly covered by the decision was the reference in any one, excess in any one item po. Yun po yung reference to Section 1A.

**[01:26:42.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001905)  
So, the initial view, Your Honor, is pwede pa namang kung may excess but the excess is to be determined from the total target.

**[01:26:51.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001906)  
Yun po yung initial reading po ng team doon sa decision, subject nga po doon sa mapag-uusapan on the items to be clarified or to be respected.

**[01:27:02.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001907)  
But yung 1B is the totality. Diba, 1B is new revenue collection arising from taxes or non-tax.

**[01:27:13.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001908)  
1B is new, Your Honor.

**[01:27:14.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001909)  
Oo.

**[01:27:15.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001910)  
New over and above the PSF levels.

**[01:27:18.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001911)  
Yes. Oo. So, etatlo lang naman yung sa special provision, diba?

**[01:27:24.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001912)  
That is correct. There are only three triggers.

**[01:27:26.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001913)  
Three triggers lang. Kung tinanggal mo yung dalawa, matitira yung foreign assistant.

**[01:27:30.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001914)  
In other words, the...

**[01:27:32.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001915)  
The unprogrammed is only designed for foreign assistant.

**[01:27:36.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001916)  
Now, the point I'm trying to make is we don't want to approve a budget here that is unconstitutional.

**[01:27:43.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001917)  
That's our...

**[01:27:44.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001918)

**[01:27:56.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001919)  
For sure, tatanggalin na namin yung 1A and 1B.

**[01:28:00.440] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001920)  
Your Honor, as for the...

**[01:28:02.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001921)

**[01:28:08.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001922)  
Probably, pwedeng i-correct yung base. That's an initial reading.

**[01:28:13.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001923)  
So, mag-i-introduce ulit ng isa pang special provision.

**[01:28:19.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001924)  
Kasi those are the triggers. Without those triggers, you cannot trigger the unprogrammed.

**[01:28:25.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001925)  
The source of the unprogrammed is new revenue.

**[01:28:28.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001926)  
That is correct, Your Honor.

**[01:28:29.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001927)  
Or excess revenue.

**[01:28:30.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001928)  
Excess revenue in any one...

**[01:28:32.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001929)  
Tax or non-tax.

**[01:28:34.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001930)  
That is correct, Your Honor.

**[01:28:35.400] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001931)  
We commit to work with the Senate and Congress to fine-tune the special provision for unprogrammed to make sure we will fully subscribe and follow the Supreme Court decision.

**[01:45:00.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002909)  
Quickly mention, Your Honor, the seven pillars.

**[01:45:02.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002910)  
Budget reliability, transparency of public finances, management of assets and liabilities,

**[01:45:08.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002911)  
fiscal strategy and budgeting, predictability and control in execution,

**[01:45:13.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002912)  
accounting and reporting, and external scrutiny and audit.

**[01:45:16.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002913)

**[01:45:29.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002916)

**[01:45:33.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002917)

**[02:00:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003114)  
Patuloy na namomblema yung livestock, particularly yung pork and piggery sector, yung magbababoy natin on the issue of the funding for the ASF.

**[02:00:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003115)  
So we wrote the DBM according to the DA. May mga unpaid ASF and avian influenza indemnities. 58.17 million and 336.9 million.

**[02:00:39.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003116)  
Ang recommendation ng DBM is to seek the President's approval to release 7.16 million.

**[02:00:48.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003117)  
From the Contingency Fund and 329 million from the FY National Disaster Risk Reduction Fund.

**[02:00:58.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003118)  
So what is the status of the, after your recommendation, has the request of the approval been relayed to the President?

**[02:01:10.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003119)  
Yes, Your Honor. We have communicated formally our recommendation to the President.

**[02:01:16.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003120)  
And after this meeting, we'll communicate.

**[02:01:18.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003121)  
We'll communicate directly with the Office of the Executive Secretary to follow up on the status so that we can facilitate the immediate release of the allotments to DA to make sure that we'll be able to pay the farm.

**[02:01:28.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003122)  
Thank you. And we will monitor that and I will ask that again during plenary because talagang tinatamaan ang ating livestock sector, particularly ang mga magbababoy dahil mas mababa na ang farmgate price sa production, cost of production.

**[02:01:48.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003123)  
And the indemnities will go a long way for them given the current challenges.

**[02:01:58.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003124)  
We've also asked and we'll get a sense, the DA to consider asking the President to already declare a state of calamity.

**[02:02:09.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003125)  
Right now, five regions in the country have reported about 7 billion pesos losses in El Nino.

**[02:02:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003127)  
And it will get worse before it gets worse.

**[02:02:28.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003128)  
And so we would like to get the sense of the DBM Secretary. Would you support that declaration and would there be funds from the national government available?

**[02:02:45.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003129)  
Well, Your Honor, the declaration of a state.

**[02:02:48.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003130)  
of calamity arising from the extraordinary El Nino will not just unlock national government resources.

**[02:02:55.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003131)  
In fact, it will also unlock local disaster risk reduction and management funds.

**[02:03:00.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003132)  
So for us to have a whole of government and whole of nation approach in responding to El Nino, certainly we would welcome, should the President declare a state of calamity,

**[02:03:12.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003133)  
we stand ready in guiding the agencies how they can best utilize their funding to respond to El Nino.

**[02:03:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003134)  
But do you have funds?

**[02:03:20.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003135)  
For El Nino, Your Honor, apart from the regular N-DREAM, the National Disaster Risk Reduction Management Fund,

**[02:03:29.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003136)  
agencies have initially identified as well portions of their respective budgets that will be used for the preparation or response to El Nino.

**[02:03:39.080] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003137)  
Based on the data at hand, we have about 112 for the remaining part of the 2026 GAA, 112 billion.

**[02:03:48.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003138)  
And another 104 billion for the 2027 NEP.

**[02:03:54.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003139)  
This is exclusive, Your Honor, of the Local Disaster Risk Reduction and Management Fund that may be accessed once there is this declaration.

**[02:04:03.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003140)  
But even absent, Your Honor, a national declaration, LGUs who have declared their respective state of calamity because of their mandate under Republic Act 10-121

**[02:04:14.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003141)  
can already access their respective local disaster funds for this.

**[02:04:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003142)  
Actually, may mga local declarations na.

**[02:04:21.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003143)  
In Regions 5, Calbar Zone, Region 2, Soxargen, and one more.

**[02:04:32.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003144)  
I forget.

**[02:04:33.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003145)  
Region 5. Did I say Region 5?

**[02:04:36.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003146)  
Okay.

**[02:04:37.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003147)  
Region 2, Region 5, Calbar Zone, Soxargen, and Caraga.

**[02:04:42.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003148)  
There you go.

**[02:04:42.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003149)  
So five have already locally.

**[02:04:44.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003150)  
But, you know.

**[02:04:46.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003151)  
So you're saying there is 120?

**[02:04:48.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003152)  
122 billion for 2026.

**[02:04:51.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003153)  
112 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:04:53.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003154)  
112 billion still available for this year.

**[02:04:57.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003155)  
This is the totality, Your Honor, that includes the 39 billion in NRIP fund.

**[02:05:02.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003156)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:05:03.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003157)  
Yes.

**[02:05:03.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003158)  
So it's the last quarter.

**[02:05:09.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003159)  
We can make that declaration and we have the funds to provide for the support.

**[02:05:15.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003160)  
Okay.

**[02:05:17.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003161)  
So thank you.

**[02:05:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003162)  
Thank you for that clarification.

**[02:05:22.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003163)  
The other one would be the...

**[02:05:24.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003164)

**[02:05:28.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003166)

**[02:05:31.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003167)

**[02:05:46.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003170)

**[02:05:52.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003173)

**[02:05:54.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003174)

**[02:06:02.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003175)

**[02:06:11.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003176)

**[02:06:17.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003177)

**[02:06:18.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003178)

**[02:06:24.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003181)  
From the initial 500 million, this is what came from?

**[02:06:29.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003182)  
And the reason why I'm raising this is because I had spoken with Deputy Executive Secretary Andres.

**[02:06:41.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003183)  
And he informed me that for the council to be effective, he needs...

**[02:06:48.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003184)

**[02:06:51.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003185)

**[02:06:55.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003186)  
Actually, Your Honor, if I may respond.

**[02:06:57.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003187)

**[02:07:08.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003188)

**[02:07:13.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003189)  
And I believe they are now organizing the permanent secretariat.

**[02:07:18.400] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003193)

**[02:07:22.780] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003194)  
So this is over and above what was initially quoted, which is the 35.74 million,

**[02:07:28.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003195)  
which is just part of the regular operating budget of the OP.

**[02:07:32.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003196)  
So with the approval on the creation of the positions, Your Honor,

**[02:07:35.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003197)  
once they fill that up, we will be releasing additional allotments to cover the salaries of the hired personnel, Your Honor.

**[02:07:43.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003198)  
Okay. Thank you.

**[02:07:45.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003200)  
Because as I mentioned,

**[02:07:47.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003201)

**[02:08:46.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003214)

**[02:08:47.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003215)

**[02:08:51.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003216)

**[02:08:51.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003217)

**[02:08:54.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003218)

**[02:08:57.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003219)  
So this AAES funding is critical

**[02:09:02.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003220)

**[02:09:08.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003221)

**[02:09:12.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003222)  
So, okay.

**[02:09:14.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003223)  
Siguro yung huli natin,

**[02:09:15.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003224)  
just going back to the earlier LGSF,

**[02:09:19.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003225)  
you know, I remember kasi during the time of Secretary Butchabad,

**[02:09:22.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003226)  
they were strong, and Secretary Jesse Robredo,

**[02:09:26.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003227)

**[02:09:32.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003228)

**[02:09:43.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003229)  
Your Honor, actually, there is one...

**[02:09:45.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003230)  
There is one sub-program under the LGSF,

**[02:09:48.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003231)  
which is what we call the Support for Participatory Budgeting,

**[02:09:51.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003232)  
although it's only 1 billion out of the total LGSF.

**[02:09:55.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003233)  
So we intend to use this really to also incentivize public participation,

**[02:10:01.700] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003234)  
CSO participation in the budget.

**[02:10:03.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003235)  
And for 2027, this is also one thing that we would like to use

**[02:10:09.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003236)  
to incentivize LGUs who have actively...

**[02:10:15.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003237)  
Participated in the Open Government Partnership,

**[02:10:17.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003238)  
almost similar to the concept of the bottom-up budgeting implemented before, Your Honor.

**[02:10:22.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003239)  
Okay, and if you can provide us, you know, some material on this

**[02:10:26.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003240)  
so that we can, likewise, you know, if we need to fund more, why not?

**[02:10:32.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003241)  
Certainly, Your Honor.

**[02:10:34.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003242)  
Now, on the issue of the...

Okay.

Dapat yun.

**[02:11:16.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003255)

**[02:12:40.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003287)  
of LGSF funds.

**[02:12:44.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003288)  
And they were able to buy...

**[02:12:45.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003289)

So, I hope, you know,

**[02:12:52.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003291)

**[02:12:55.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003292)

So, hopefully, as you implement this program

Right now, Your Honor,

**[02:13:13.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003299)  
we are working with the DILG,

**[02:13:15.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003300)

**[02:13:20.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003301)

**[02:13:23.900] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003302)

**[02:13:26.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003303)  
So, your LGSF fund will go...

**[02:13:33.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003306)

**[02:13:35.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003307)  
So, your fund will...

**[02:13:38.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003308)

**[02:13:41.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003309)

**[02:13:43.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003310)

**[02:13:45.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003311)  
The PIDIG Multipurpose Cooperative

**[02:13:48.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003312)

**[02:13:52.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003313)

**[02:13:56.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003314)

**[02:13:59.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003315)  
San Peregrina

**[02:14:00.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003316)  
was about 68 million pesos worth of rice.

**[02:14:08.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003317)  
Diba?

**[02:14:09.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003318)  
So, malaking pondo na malaking pakinabang doon sa lokal,

**[02:14:13.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003319)

**[02:14:15.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003320)  
Everybody wins.

**[02:14:17.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003321)  
The one that doesn't win is the corrupt tongpats kickback.

**[02:14:23.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003322)  
You know?

**[02:14:25.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003323)

**[02:14:27.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003324)  
Diba?

**[02:14:27.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003325)

**[02:14:31.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003326)  
Diba?

**[02:14:32.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003327)  
Yun ang loser doon.

**[02:14:33.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003328)  
Kaya, bantayan ninyo para win-win.

**[02:14:38.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003329)  
Ika nga, our Senate President.

**[02:14:41.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003330)

**[02:14:42.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003331)

Before plenary, you don't have to do it.

**[02:14:58.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003337)  
Yes, thank you.

**[02:14:58.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003338)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[03:00:19.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005476)  
Yes, I also wanted to inquire about the growth equity fund.

**[03:00:24.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005477)

**[03:00:28.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005478)

**[03:00:34.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005479)

**[03:00:39.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005480)

**[03:00:46.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005481)  
and 1.5% of the 2026 appropriation.

**[03:00:51.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005482)  
This fund was intended for disadvantaged LGUs

**[03:00:56.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005483)

**[03:00:58.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005484)

**[03:01:04.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005485)  
Kinawawa yung mga kawawang-kawawa na.

**[03:01:07.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005486)  
Diba, ganun ang nangyari dyan.

**[03:01:08.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005487)  
Ang requirements continue to prevent implementation

**[03:01:11.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005488)  
and 0.4% could not be more dismal.

**[03:01:26.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005489)  
No, Secretary.

**[03:01:28.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005490)  
We're not trying to blame you for what happened in the past

**[03:01:31.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005491)  
but you realize, of course, that there's a proposed 10.3 billion for 2027

**[03:01:36.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005492)  
and we don't understand now what requirements are going to be necessary

**[03:01:42.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005493)  
for implementation.

**[03:01:43.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005494)  
Otherwise, we'll be stuck at less than 1% implementation.

**[03:01:47.920] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005495)  
Well, we note the observation of the good senator

**[03:01:50.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005496)

**[03:01:54.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005497)

**[03:01:58.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005498)

**[03:02:02.960] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005499)  
So, what we can assure the committee is that

**[03:02:05.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005500)  
this should not happen as well again in 2027, Your Honor,

**[03:02:09.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005501)  
so that LGUs...

**[03:02:10.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005502)  
How to do that?

**[03:02:11.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005503)  
So that the guidelines can be issued...

**[03:02:15.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005504)  
Are the guidelines already in place?

**[03:02:18.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005505)  
We have the guidelines.

**[03:02:19.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005506)  
But these are the old ones.

**[03:02:20.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005507)  
Baka baguhin para sa 2027.

**[03:02:22.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005508)  
Mag-iintay na naman ang 2028.

**[03:02:25.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005509)  
Pambihira naman.

**[03:02:26.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005510)  
If there will be no substantial changes,

**[03:02:28.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005511)  
and other mandates from Congress,

**[03:02:29.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005512)  
certainly, Your Honor,

**[03:02:30.860] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005513)

**[03:02:33.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005514)

**[03:02:36.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005515)

**[03:02:37.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005516)

**[03:02:38.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005517)  
We assure the committee, Your Honor,

**[03:02:40.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005518)

**[03:02:43.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005519)

**[03:02:45.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005520)  
O, kasi nakakaawa itong nangyari.

**[03:02:50.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005521)  
Antay sila ng antay.

**[03:02:51.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005522)  
Sila na nga yung pinakamahirap.

**[03:02:53.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005523)  
Mas lalo silang kinawawa.

**[03:02:55.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005524)  
Huwag po kayong mag-alala, Your Honor,

**[03:02:56.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005525)

**[03:02:57.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005526)  
Bawal po ang mabagal

**[03:02:58.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005527)  
ngayon.

**[03:02:59.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005528)  
Sure ka, ha?

**[03:03:00.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005530)

**[03:03:03.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005531)

**[03:03:14.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005535)

**[03:03:19.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-005536)

**[03:03:44.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005544)  
Kuha-kuha nyo po, Your Honor,

**[03:03:46.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005545)

**[03:03:48.080] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005546)

**[03:03:51.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005547)

**[03:03:54.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005548)  
at isa po sa naging change

**[03:03:55.920] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005549)

**[03:03:56.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005550)

**[03:03:59.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005551)

**[03:04:01.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005552)

**[03:04:04.860] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005553)

**[03:04:07.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005555)

**[03:04:09.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005556)

**[03:04:12.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005557)  
but we just eliminated

**[03:04:13.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005558)

**[03:04:18.860] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005559)  
And in fact, if I may report,

**[03:04:21.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005560)  
over the last two months,

**[03:04:22.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005561)  
hindi po kami tumitigil ngayon

**[03:04:24.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005562)

**[03:04:25.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005563)

**[03:04:27.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005564)

**[03:04:34.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005567)

**[03:04:37.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005569)

**[03:04:49.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005576)  
And we will furnish the committee

**[03:04:51.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005577)

**[03:04:53.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005578)

**[03:14:47.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005875)

**[03:14:49.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005876)

**[03:14:51.080] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005877)

**[03:14:53.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005878)

**[03:14:55.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005879)

**[03:14:56.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005880)

**[03:14:58.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005881)

**[03:15:00.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005882)

**[03:15:18.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005883)

**[03:15:29.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005884)

**[03:17:52.480] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005908)

**[03:18:01.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005909)

**[03:20:20.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005949)

**[03:20:34.440] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005956)

**[03:20:38.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005958)

**[03:20:41.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005959)

**[03:20:43.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005960)

**[03:20:45.760] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005961)  
So, maganda pong tignan yun

**[03:20:47.080] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005962)

**[03:20:48.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-005963)

**[03:20:49.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005964)  
Yes.

**[03:20:50.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005965)  
In fact, mayroon rin proposal kami

**[03:20:52.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005966)

**[03:20:55.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005967)

**[03:20:57.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005968)

**[03:20:58.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005969)

**[03:21:00.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005970)

**[03:21:01.820] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005971)

**[03:21:04.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005972)  
Kung lahat nasa local government na lang,

**[03:21:06.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005973)  
total, you can capacitate the local government.

**[03:21:08.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005974)  
Like I said,

**[03:21:09.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005975)  
the building,

**[03:21:10.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005976)

**[03:21:12.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005977)

**[03:21:14.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005978)

**[03:21:15.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005979)

**[03:21:21.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005981)

**[03:21:24.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005983)

**[03:21:27.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005984)

**[03:21:28.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005985)

**[03:21:31.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005986)

**[03:21:33.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005987)  
we're forecasting that yung NTA

**[03:21:53.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005994)  
Oo.

**[03:21:54.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005995)

**[03:21:56.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005996)

**[03:21:58.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005997)

**[03:22:01.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005998)

Anong average growth ng

For 2025,

the actual release is

**[03:23:19.620] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-006027)  
124

**[03:23:20.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-006028)  
for military.
